Emoji
Reaction emoji and emoji inside messages render with the platform emoji font: Noto Color Emoji on Android (SIL Open Font License 1.1, shipped with Android) and the operating system's emoji font in each supported browser. No emoji font is bundled, so an emoji can look different on Android and on a desktop browser; this is accepted (D-131). The reaction palette is the 8 emoji of D-22 and, from V2, the 4 extra emoji of D-72.
| Palette position | Name in D-22 or D-72 | Character | Accessibility label |
| 1 | thumbs up | U+1F44D | Thumbs up |
| 2 | heart | U+2764 U+FE0F | Heart |
| 3 | laughing | U+1F602 | Laughing |
| 4 | surprised | U+1F62E | Surprised |
| 5 | crying | U+1F622 | Crying |
| 6 | folded hands | U+1F64F | Folded hands |
| 7 | fire | U+1F525 | Fire |
| 8 | party popper | U+1F389 | Party popper |
| 9 (V2, extra_reactions) | star | U+2B50 | Star |
| 10 (V2, extra_reactions) | hundred points | U+1F4AF | Hundred points |
| 11 (V2, extra_reactions) | clapping hands | U+1F44F | Clapping hands |
| 12 (V2, extra_reactions) | eyes | U+1F440 | Eyes |
---TABLE END---

Assumption: the characters in this table are the ones the reactions.emoji CHECK of M1-01 and M5-08 lists; the CHECK is authoritative. The palette is exported once from packages/shared (config module, M2-01), and M1-01 checks that the migration lists the same characters; if they differ, this table is corrected to match the CHECK.
Avatars
Shape: circle (radius.full). Sizes: 24 (Profile tab and rail item), 28 (beside the last received bubble of a group), 40 (top bar of a chat, admin), 48 (forward picker, member list), 56 (chat row), 64 (settings profile card), 96 (chat info header).
MVP: initials only, because avatar_url stays empty (D-72). Initials are the first code point of the first two words of the display name, uppercased with the device locale and counted with Array.from (D-116); one word gives one letter. Font Inter SemiBold 600 at 40% of the avatar size, color #FFFFFF, fill from the avatar fill list (12.2).
Groups: initials from the group title on the fill chosen by chat_id. Saved Messages: the chat.saved icon on color.brand.primary. A "Deleted account" (D-110) uses person.deleted and a "Former member" (D-89) uses person.former, both on color.badge.muted.bg with the icon in color.badge.muted.text.
From V2 (D-72): the avatar image fills the circle with object-fit cover; the initials avatar shows while the signed URL loads and if it fails. The image is decorative for screen readers because the name is always next to it.
V2 premium badge (D-72, D-87): person.badge at icon.xs in color.brand.primary, 4 to the right of the display name, with accessibility label "Premium".
Attachment tiles and imagery
Photos are shown from the signed URL of the stored object (D-113); DoloGram stores no thumbnails, so the tile shows the full image scaled down. A tile keeps its size before the image loads, so the list does not jump.
Single photo or video in a bubble: width 240, height from the stored image aspect ratio clamped between 120 and 320; if the ratio is not known yet, 240 by 180. Several photos and videos: a grid of square tiles in 2 columns, each (240 - 2) / 2 = 119 wide, gap 2, in send order, up to 10 (D-90).
Video: DoloGram generates no video thumbnail; the tile is color.bg.surface with media.video at icon.xl in color.text.secondary and the caption "Video" with the size ("Video · 12.4 MB") in body.sm.
Voice note and file attachments render as rows inside the bubble, not as tiles (CMP-11).
No stock imagery, illustrations or remote images are used anywhere. Images in Markdown are never fetched; they render as their alt text (D-99).
12.4 Components
Each component has an identifier CMP-nn, used by the screen templates (12.5) and the implementation mapping (12.10). The six states are: Default; Pressed (finger down on Android, mouse down or touch on web; hover on web uses the same treatment at half the duration); Focused (keyboard focus on web and admin; Android TalkBack focus uses the system highlight); Disabled; Loading; Error. A state that cannot occur for a component reads "N/A:" with the reason. Every string in quotes is the initial value of its key in the strings module (D-35, D-95, D-127).
Shared state rules for every interactive component: Pressed fills with color.bg.pressed (or color.brand.pressed on a primary fill) for motion.duration.fast. Focused draws a 2 px color.focus.ring outline with a 2 px offset, following the component radius; it is never removed for mouse users who use the keyboard (:focus-visible). Disabled keeps the layout, uses color.text.disabled for the label, ignores input and is announced as disabled; a disabled control that needs a connection shows the reason "No connection" in its helper line or sheet subtitle.
| ID | Component | Clients | Used in |
| CMP-01 | Button set | Android, web, admin | Every screen. |
| CMP-02 | Text field | Android, web, admin | AND-01, AND-02, AND-10, AND-11, AND-13; WEB-01, WEB-02, WEB-11, WEB-13; ADM-01, ADM-04, ADM-05. |
| CMP-03 | Search field | Android, web, admin | AND-07, WEB-08, ADM-03, ADM-07. |
| CMP-04 | Switch | Android, web, admin | AND-14 (Force Low Signal Mode), ADM-05 (premium, V2). |
| CMP-05 | Avatar | Android, web | Chat rows, chat header, bubbles, chat info, settings, pickers. |
| CMP-06 | Badge | Android, web | Unread count, jump-to-bottom, Archived (n) row. |
| CMP-07 | Message bubble | Android, web | AND-05, WEB-06. |
| CMP-08 | Status indicator | Android, web | Own bubbles, chat rows. |
| CMP-09 | Chat list row | Android, web | AND-03, AND-04, AND-07; WEB-04, WEB-05, WEB-08. |
| CMP-10 | Composer | Android, web | AND-05, WEB-06. |
| CMP-11 | Attachment tile | Android, web | Bubbles, composer strip, media viewer entry. |
| CMP-12 | Reactions bar and palette | Android, web | Under bubbles; message actions. |
| CMP-13 | Connection banner and Low Signal chip | Android (all three), web (offline banner only) | AND-03, AND-05; WEB-04, WEB-06. |
| CMP-14 | Folder tabs | Android, web | AND-03, WEB-04. |
| CMP-15 | Bottom sheet | Android, web below 768 px | New sheet, AND-08, chat row actions, Mute options, Theme. |
| CMP-16 | Dialog | Android, web, admin | Confirmations, web New chat, New group, Forward, Folders, Settings. |
| CMP-17 | Toast | Android, web, admin | Short confirmations and non-blocking errors. |
| CMP-18 | Skeleton | Android, web, admin | Chat list, chat, admin tables. |
| CMP-19 | Empty state | Android, web, admin | Empty chat, archive, folder, pickers, admin lists. |
| CMP-20 | Error state | Android, web, admin | Failed loads, denied permissions. |
| CMP-21 | Top bar | Android, web | Every user screen. |
| CMP-22 | Date separator and unread divider | Android, web | AND-05, WEB-06. |
| CMP-23 | Menu | Web from 768 px, admin | WEB-04 row menu, WEB-07 message menu, admin row actions. |
| CMP-24 to CMP-31 | Admin components | Admin | 12.9. |
| CMP-32 | Bottom tab bar | Android, web below 768 px | AND-03, AND-14; WEB-03, WEB-04, WEB-14. |
| CMP-33 | Navigation rail | Web from 768 px | WEB-03 and every web route. |
| CMP-34 | Message focus overlay | Android, web below 768 px | AND-06, WEB-07. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-01 Button set
| Part or state | Specification |
| Variants | Primary: fill color.brand.primary, label color.brand.onPrimary. Secondary: fill color.bg.app, 1 px color.border.strong outline, label color.text.primary. Text: no fill or outline, label color.brand.primary. Danger: fill color.danger, label color.danger.onDanger. Icon: a 24 icon centered in a 40 circle with no fill, icon color.text.primary. |
| Size | Height 48 on Android, 44 on the user web, 36 in the admin web; horizontal padding space.6 (20); minimum width 88; radius.md. Icon button: 40 visual circle inside a 48 (Android) or 44 (web) target, 32 visual and target in the admin web. Full-width buttons on sign-in, change password and below 768 px in dialogs. |
| Content | Label in label.lg, sentence case, one line, ellipsis if it does not fit; an optional icon.md 8 before the label. Order in a row: the dismissive action left (Cancel), the confirming action right. |
| Default | As in Variants. |
| Pressed | Primary: fill color.brand.pressed. Secondary, Text and Icon: fill color.bg.pressed. Danger: the danger fill with a 12% #000000 overlay. |
| Focused | 2 px color.focus.ring outline, 2 px offset, radius.md (radius.full for Icon). |
| Disabled | Primary and Danger: fill color.bg.disabled, label color.text.disabled. Secondary: outline color.border.subtle, label color.text.disabled. Text and Icon: label or icon color.text.disabled. |
| Loading | After motion.delay.loading the label is replaced by a 20 spinner (status.sending icon rotating) in the label color; the button keeps its width, ignores presses and is announced busy (accessibilityState busy, aria-busy="true"). |
| Error | N/A: a button has no error state; the error appears in the form (CMP-02) or as a toast (CMP-17). |
---TABLE END---

CMP-02 Text field
| Part or state | Specification |
| Anatomy | Label above (label.md, color.text.primary, 6 below it); field; helper line below (body.sm, color.text.secondary, 4 above it); optional counter right-aligned on the helper line ("12 / 64"). |
| Size | Height 48 on Android, 44 on web, 36 in the admin web; multi-line fields grow to 6 lines. Padding 12 horizontal; radius.md; input text body.lg (16, so mobile browsers do not zoom), placeholder color.text.secondary. |
| Default | Fill color.bg.app, 1 px color.border.strong outline. |
| Pressed | Same as Focused; the caret appears. |
| Focused | 2 px color.brand.primary outline (replaces the 1 px outline, no layout shift: the inner padding shrinks by 1). On web the focus ring is the brand outline itself. |
| Disabled | Fill color.bg.surface, 1 px color.border.subtle outline, text color.text.disabled; helper says why when the reason is the connection ("No connection"). |
| Loading | While its form submits, the field is read-only with the Disabled look; the spinner belongs to the submit button (CMP-01). |
| Error | 2 px color.danger outline; the helper line is replaced by status.failed at icon.xs and the message in color.danger. The message is announced (accessibilityLiveRegion polite, aria-live="polite") and linked to the field (aria-describedby). |
| Password | A trailing icon button with action.showPassword or action.hidePassword, labels "Show password" and "Hide password". |
| Length limits | Counter appears when the limit is 64 or less (group title 1 to 64, D-109; folder name 1 to 32, D-24; display name 1 to 64, D-89) and counts code points (D-116). Above the limit the counter turns color.danger and the confirming button is disabled. |
| Validation strings | "Enter a valid email address." (D-116); "Use at least 12 characters." (D-47); "Passwords don't match."; "Enter a name of 1 to 64 characters."; "Enter a name of 1 to 32 characters."; "Use 5 to 32 lowercase letters, digits or _, starting with a letter." (D-89). |
---TABLE END---

CMP-03 Search field
| Part or state | Specification |
| Anatomy | action.search icon (icon.md, color.text.secondary) 12 from the left, input, and a clear button (nav.close, label "Clear search") when the field has text. |
| Size | Height 40 (admin 36), radius.full, fill color.bg.surface, no outline, input body.md, placeholder in color.text.secondary: "Search chats" (AND-07, WEB-08), "Search by username or email" (ADM-03), "Filter by target" (ADM-07). |
| Behavior | Filters as the user types, after 150 ms without a keystroke. In the forward picker, it matches the displayed chat title, case-insensitive, by substring, on the device only; DoloGram has no message or global search (D-80). In ADM-03 the match is partial and case-insensitive on username or email (11.4). |
| Default | As in Size. |
| Pressed | Same as Focused. |
| Focused | 2 px color.brand.primary outline, radius.full. |
| Disabled | N/A: the search field filters local data and is never disabled. |
| Loading | Admin only: the table below shows its skeleton (CMP-18); the field itself does not change. |
| Error | N/A: a search with no match shows the empty state of the list ("No chats match", "No accounts match"). |

CMP-04 Switch
| Part or state | Specification |
| Anatomy | Row with the label on the left (body.lg), an optional helper line (body.sm, color.text.secondary), and the switch on the right. The whole row is the touch target. |
| Size | Track 48 by 28, radius.full; thumb 22 circle with 3 inset; row minimum height 56 on Android, 48 on web. |
| Default | Off: track color.border.strong, thumb #FFFFFF in light and color.text.primary in dark. On: track color.brand.primary, thumb color.brand.onPrimary, thumb moved right over motion.duration.base with motion.easing.standard. |
| Pressed | The row fills with color.bg.pressed. |
| Focused | 2 px color.focus.ring outline around the track, 2 px offset. |
| Disabled | Track color.bg.disabled, thumb color.text.disabled, label color.text.disabled. |
| Loading | Admin premium switches (V2) only: while the change is saved the thumb is replaced by a 16 spinner and the switch ignores input; the value changes only after success. |
| Error | The switch returns to its previous value and a toast shows the error text with Retry (D-95). |
| Accessibility | Role switch with the checked state (accessibilityRole "switch", role="switch" with aria-checked); the label names the setting, for example "Force Low Signal Mode". |
| Force Low Signal Mode helper | "Compress photos and wait for Wi-Fi before sending media." (LS-5); Android only (D-19). |

CMP-05 Avatar
| Part or state | Specification |
| Spec | Sizes, initials and fills as 12.3. |
| Default | Initials avatar or, from V2, the image. |
| Pressed | Only where the avatar opens something (chat header avatar opens chat info): color.bg.pressed circle 4 larger than the avatar behind it. |
| Focused | Where pressable: 2 px color.focus.ring circle, 2 px offset. |
| Disabled | N/A: an avatar is never disabled. |
| Loading | V2 image: the initials avatar shows until the image loads. |
| Error | V2 image: if the signed URL fails twice, the initials avatar stays. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-06 Badge
| Part or state | Specification |
| Unread badge | Height 20, minimum width 20, horizontal padding 6, radius.full; label.sm with weight 600; value 1 to 99, then "99+". Fill color.brand.primary with color.brand.onPrimary; in a muted chat color.badge.muted.bg with color.badge.muted.text (D-94). |
| Count chip | The "Archived (n)" row and the folder tab counts do not use a badge; the count is part of the text. |
| Jump-to-bottom badge | The unread badge placed with its center on the top-right of the 40 button. |
| Accessibility | The badge is not focusable; its parent row includes the count in its label ("3 unread messages"). |
| Pressed, Focused, Disabled, Loading, Error | N/A: a badge is not interactive and has no loading or error state. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-07 Message bubble
| Part or state | Specification |
| Layout | Own messages align right, received messages align left. Maximum bubble width: 78% of the message list width, and at most 560 on web. Padding 8 vertical, 12 horizontal; radius.bubble (22) on every corner and no tail. Inside a group the sender-side corners where two bubbles meet use radius.bubbleJoin (4): the first bubble its bottom sender-side corner, a middle bubble both sender-side corners, the last bubble its top sender-side corner. A group is consecutive messages of one sender less than 5 minutes apart; gap space.1 inside a group and space.3 between groups. |
| Sent (own) | Fill color.bubble.out.bg; text body.lg in color.bubble.out.text; links underlined in color.bubble.out.text; inline code in mono on color.bubble.out.quote. |
| Received | Fill color.bubble.in.bg; text body.lg in color.text.primary; links underlined in color.brand.primary; inline code in mono on color.bubble.in.quote. The last bubble of a received group has a 28 avatar 8 to its left, in direct and group chats; other received bubbles keep a 36 left inset so the column stays aligned. In a group chat the sender display name sits above the first bubble of a group, outside it, in label.sm color.text.secondary, 12 from the bubble's left edge (D-143). |
| Time | No time inside the bubble (D-143). Time rows (CMP-22) give the time at the start of each day and above the first message after a gap of 60 minutes or more. The time of one message, in the device locale (D-109), shows in body.sm color.text.secondary under the bubble on its side for 3000 ms after a tap on the bubble (Android, web below 768 px), and in a tooltip after 500 ms of hover on web from 768 px. The status of own messages sits under the bubble (CMP-08). |
| Markdown | Bold, italic, code and links only (D-30); other constructs as plain text of their children and images as alt text (D-99); HTML as source text (D-05). Code blocks: mono, padding 8, radius.sm, the quote fill of the bubble, horizontal scroll inside the bubble. |
| Reply quote | Top of the bubble, above the text: a block with a 3 wide bar on its left (color.bubble.out.text on own bubbles, color.brand.primary on received), fill color.bubble.out.quote or color.bubble.in.quote, radius 8, padding 6 vertical and 8 horizontal; the parent sender name in label.md and one line of the parent text in body.sm with ellipsis; an attachment-only parent shows its preview word ("Photo", "Video", "Voice note", "File"). A deleted parent shows "Deleted message" in italics (D-22). A tap scrolls to the parent and fills it with color.bg.selected for motion.duration.highlight; if the parent is not loaded, older pages load first, with a spinner in the quote. |
| Forwarded text | Renders exactly as a sent or received bubble with no "Forwarded" label and no original sender, because a forward is a new message with copied text and no stored attribution (D-31, D-133). The forward flow itself gives feedback: the notice "Attachments are not forwarded yet" in the picker for a message with attachments (D-50) and the toast "Forwarded" after the item is queued. |
| Edited marker | The word "edited" under the bubble on its side, in body.sm color.text.secondary, always visible (D-22); before the status caption on own messages ("edited · Sent"). Accessibility label of the bubble adds "edited". |
| Deleted placeholder | The bubble keeps its side and fill; content is chat.deleted at icon.xs plus "Deleted message" in body.md italic (color.bubble.out.meta on own bubbles, color.text.secondary on received). It shows no reactions and no reply quote, and long press and right-click open nothing (D-22). |
| Attachment placeholder | One tile per attachment that has no message_media row yet (D-90): the size of the default tile for its type, fill color.bg.surface (received) or color.bubble.out.quote (own), an icon (action.gallery, media.video, action.record or action.file) and "Waiting for upload" in body.sm. |
| V2 kinds | Sticker: 160 by 160 image with no bubble fill; Location: a card with the coordinates, "Accuracy <n> m" and an "Open in Maps" text button (D-49); Poll: question in title.sm, one row per option with a radio, the option text, the count and the voter names (D-59); Secret chat bubbles add admin.lock at icon.xs at the end of the text; a self-destruct bubble shows the time left ("59 s", "58 min", "23 h", "6 d") under the bubble on its side in body.sm color.text.secondary (D-69). |
| Default | As above. |
| Pressed | Android and web below 768 px: a long press of 400 ms opens the message focus overlay (CMP-34, AND-06). A double tap toggles the user's own heart reaction (palette position 2, D-22) and shows a 64 heart emoji at the bubble center that scales from 0.6 to 1 and fades out over motion.duration.slow (fade only with reduced motion); offline, or with 3 active reactions and no heart among them, the double tap changes nothing and shows the toast "No connection" or "You can add up to 3 reactions" (D-22). It does nothing on deleted messages and in Secret Chats (D-68). Web from 768 px: hover shows a row of three 32 icon buttons 4 outside the bubble on the side away from the sender: React (action.react, label "React"), Reply (action.reply, "Reply") and More (action.more, "More actions"); a press darkens the button (D-143). |
| Focused | Web: each bubble is focusable in the message list (roving tabindex, arrow keys move between bubbles); focus draws the focus ring around the bubble, shows the hover row and the time; Enter or Shift+F10 opens the menu. |
| Disabled | N/A: a bubble is not disabled; actions inside its menu are (offline edit, delete and reactions, D-22). |
| Loading | A queued, sending or uploading own message is shown at once from the local queue with its status (LS-1). Older pages loading: a 24 spinner centered above the oldest bubble after motion.delay.loading. |
| Error | A failed own message: the bubble stays, a status.failed icon (icon.lg, color.danger) sits 8 to the left of the bubble, and under the bubble a caption row shows "Failed" in label.sm color.danger with the Text buttons Retry and Discard (D-93). Discard is disabled offline with "No connection" when the row exists (LS-7). |
| Accessibility label | "<sender or You>, <text or attachment summary>, <time>, <status>", for example "You, See you at 6, 17:42, sent". |
---TABLE END---

CMP-08 Status indicator
Every own message has exactly one status (LS-2). The states that are not final (queued, deferred, sending and failed) show under every affected bubble, in words, with the two labels of D-17 written exactly. The final states show only under the last own message of the chat, as the word "Sent" or "Seen", so an own message with no caption is sent (D-143). Captions sit under the bubble on the right, 4 below it or below its reaction chips, in label.sm.
| State | Caption under the bubble | Icon in a chat row (icon.sm) | Accessibility text |
| queued | status.queued at icon.xs and "Queued" in color.text.secondary, under each queued bubble | status.queued | Queued |
| queued, deferred under LS-5 | status.waitingWifi at icon.xs and "waiting for Wi-Fi" in color.warning.text, under each deferred bubble | status.waitingWifi | Queued, waiting for Wi-Fi |
| sending | status.sending at icon.xs rotating once per motion.duration.spinner and "Sending" in color.text.secondary | status.sending | Sending |
| sending while Low Signal Mode is active | status.sending rotating and "sending (low signal)" in color.warning.text | status.sending | Sending (low signal) |
| sent | "Sent" in color.text.secondary under the last own message only; none under earlier sent messages | status.sent | Sent |
| sent and seen (D-92) | "Seen" in color.text.secondary under the last own message only | status.seen | Seen |
| failed | status.failed in color.danger beside the bubble at icon.lg (CMP-07 Error) and "Failed" with Retry and Discard, under each failed bubble | status.failed in color.danger | Failed. Retry or discard. |
---TABLE END---

In a chat row the icon sits before the preview and uses color.text.secondary, except failed in color.danger; the captions are not shown in rows (11.2 AND-03).
The accessibility label of every own bubble includes its status, also when no caption is drawn ("sent").
A status change cross-fades over motion.duration.fast; when a later own message becomes the last one, "Sent" or "Seen" moves to it with the same fade, and the bubbles do not move.
Web clients never show "waiting for Wi-Fi" or "sending (low signal)", because compression and deferral are mobile-only (D-19).
Pressed, Focused, Disabled, Loading, Error: N/A for the indicator itself; Retry and Discard are CMP-01 Text buttons with their own states.
CMP-09 Chat list row
| Part or state | Specification |
| Layout | Height 72; padding 16 horizontal and 8 vertical; 56 avatar; 12 gap. Line 1: name in title.sm (color.text.primary, one line, ellipsis), then the mute icon. Line 2: status icon (own last message), the preview in body.md color.text.secondary (one line, ellipsis), then " · " and the time of the newest message, which never truncates (today: time; yesterday: "Yesterday"; within 7 days: weekday; older: date, D-109). At the right, vertically centered: the unread badge. |
| Preview text | The message text with Markdown markers removed, or "Photo", "Video", "Voice note", "File", "Deleted message"; from V2 "Sticker", "Location", "Poll", "Encrypted message" (AND-03). In a group, the sender's display name and ": " precede it ("You: " for the user). |
| Unread | Name in Inter Bold 700; preview and time in color.text.primary at Inter SemiBold 600; unread badge CMP-06 at the right (D-92). |
| Muted | action.mute at icon.sm in color.text.secondary 4 after the name; the unread badge uses the muted colors; the row keeps its place (D-94). |
| Archived | In the Archive view (AND-04, WEB-05) rows look the same. In the main list a single row "Archived (n)" sits at the top, height 72, with action.archive at icon.lg in a 56 color.bg.surface circle in place of the avatar. |
| Typing | The preview is replaced by "<name> is typing..." or "<n> people are typing..." (D-118) in body.md color.brand.primary; in a direct chat by "typing...". |
| Saved Messages | Title "Saved Messages" and the chat.saved avatar (F7). |
| Default | Fill color.bg.app. |
| Pressed | Fill color.bg.pressed. Long press of 400 ms on Android opens the row actions sheet (Archive or Unarchive, Mute, Add to folder, Leave group for groups) (AND-03). |
| Focused | Web: inset 2 px color.focus.ring outline. The row is one focusable element; the context-menu key, Shift+F10 or the row's action.more button (shown on hover and focus) opens the row menu. |
| Selected | Web: the open chat row has fill color.bg.selected and aria-current="page". |
| Disabled | N/A for the row; the archive, mute and folder actions in its menu are disabled offline with "No connection" (D-24). |
| Loading | First load with no cache: 8 skeleton rows (CMP-18) after motion.delay.loading. |
| Error | N/A for a row; a failed list load shows the cached list and a toast with Retry, or the error state if there is no cache. |
| Accessibility label | "<name>, <n> unread, muted, <preview>, <time>", leaving out the parts that do not apply. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-10 Composer
| Part or state | Specification |
| Layout | A bar fixed to the bottom of the chat, above the system navigation bar (Android) or the window edge (web), fill color.bg.app, no border, padding 8. It holds one pill: fill color.bg.surface, radius.full while the input is one line and 22 when it grows, minimum height 44, padding 6. Inside the pill from the left: on Android the Camera button (a 32 circle in color.brand.primary with action.camera at icon.sm in color.brand.onPrimary, label "Camera", opening the camera of AND-08), on web the Attach button (action.attach); the input (body.lg, placeholder "Message", padding 8 vertical and 8 horizontal), which grows to 6 lines and then scrolls; at the right, while the text is blank and nothing is picked: on Android Voice note (action.record), Gallery (action.gallery, opening the gallery of AND-08) and Attach (action.attach, opening AND-08), on web Voice note. When the text is not blank or an attachment is picked, the right-hand buttons are replaced by the Text button "Send" in label.lg color.brand.primary (D-143). |
| Counter | From 3,900 code points (D-30, D-116) a counter "3,912 / 4,096" in label.sm color.text.secondary appears above the right end of the input; above 4,096 it turns color.danger and Send is disabled. |
| Reply bar | Above the input, height 48: a 3 wide color.brand.primary bar, "Reply to <name>" in label.md color.brand.primary, one line of the parent in body.sm color.text.secondary, and Cancel (nav.close, label "Cancel reply"). Escape cancels on web (WEB-06). |
| Edit bar | Same as the reply bar with "Edit message" and the original text; the Send button reads "Save" with the label "Save edit". Escape cancels on web. |
| Attachment strip | Above the input when items are picked: 64 by 64 tiles (CMP-11 composer tile) in a horizontal row with gap 8, and "n / 10" in label.sm at the right (D-90). |
| Voice note on Android | Press and hold the record button: recording starts, the input is replaced by a pulsing 8 color.danger dot (opacity 1 to 0.4, 1000 ms; static with reduced motion), the elapsed time "0:07" in body.md and "Slide left to cancel" in body.sm. Release sends the note to the queue; sliding 96 left cancels; recording stops at 15 minutes. |
| Voice note on web | Click starts recording with MediaRecorder (D-109); the record button becomes a stop button (label "Stop and send") and Escape cancels; the same dot and time show. |
| Keyboard on web | Enter sends, Shift+Enter adds a line; pasting or dropping files adds them to the strip (D-109). While a file is dragged over the chat, the chat area shows a 2 px dashed color.brand.primary outline and "Drop files to attach" centered in title.md. |
| Default | As above. |
| Pressed | Buttons per CMP-01; the input per CMP-02. |
| Focused | Input: no outline on touch; on keyboard focus (web) a 2 px color.focus.ring outline on the pill. Buttons per CMP-01. |
| Disabled | Send is not shown, so it cannot be pressed, while the text is blank and nothing is attached (AND-05). From V2 a restricted member sees the composer replaced by a 56 high bar with "You can't send messages until <time>" in body.md color.text.secondary centered (AND-20). Offline the composer stays enabled: messages are queued (LS-1). |
| Loading | N/A: sending never blocks the composer; the message appears in the list as queued at once (LS-1). |
| Error | The queue is full (200 unsent items, LS-7): the text stays in the input and a toast shows "Can't queue more than 200 unsent messages. Send or discard some first." A refused attachment shows a toast with its reason (CMP-11). |
---TABLE END---

Own pending upload: the local preview with a 32 spinner centered on a color.scrim overlay and "Uploading" in body.sm color.media.text; under LS-5 deferral status.waitingWifi and "waiting for Wi-Fi" replace the spinner and "Uploading". Received but not uploaded yet: the attachment placeholder of CMP-07. |
| Error | A signed URL that expired is renewed without a visible change (D-113). If the renewal fails: status.failed at icon.lg and "Couldn't load. Tap to retry." in body.sm on color.bg.surface; a tap retries. |
| Refused before queueing | Toast: "<file name> is too large. Photos and voice notes up to 10 MB, videos and files up to 50 MB." or "<file name> can't be sent. This file type isn't allowed." or "You can attach up to 10 files." (D-29, D-90). |

CMP-12 Reactions bar and palette
| Part or state | Specification |
| Reaction chips | Under the bubble, aligned to the bubble's side, gap space.2 from the bubble and 4 between chips, wrapping to more lines. Chip: height 28, padding 8 horizontal, radius.full, the emoji at 16 and the count in label.sm color.text.primary; fill color.bg.surface with 1 px color.border.subtle. A chip that includes the user's own reaction: fill color.bg.selected, 1 px color.brand.primary outline, count in color.brand.primary. A tap toggles the user's own reaction (AND-05). Accessibility label "<name>, <n> reactions, you reacted" or "<name>, <n> reactions". |
| Palette | A row of the 8 emoji of D-22 in the order of 12.3, each a 48 (Android) or 44 (web) square button with the emoji at 28; from V2 a 1 by 24 color.border.subtle divider and the 4 extra emoji for users with extra_reactions (D-72). On Android and web below 768 px it is the reaction pill of the message focus overlay (CMP-34); on web from 768 px a popover (CMP-23) opened by the React hover button, and above the message menu when More opens it. |
| Default | Emoji on transparent fill. |
| Pressed | 40 circle of color.bg.pressed behind the emoji; the emoji scales to 1.15 over motion.duration.fast (no scale with reduced motion). |
| Focused | 2 px color.focus.ring circle. |
| Active | An emoji the user already reacted with: 40 circle of color.bg.selected behind it; selecting it again removes the reaction (removed_at, D-22). |
| Disabled | Offline: the whole palette is disabled (emoji at 40% opacity) and its subtitle reads "No connection" (D-22). With 3 active reactions the other emoji are disabled at 40% opacity and the subtitle reads "You can add up to 3 reactions" (D-22). Not shown at all in Secret Chats and on deleted messages (D-68, D-22). |
| Loading | N/A: a reaction is written directly and its chip updates when the change arrives; no spinner. |
| Error | If the write is rejected, the chip returns to its previous state and a toast shows the error text with Retry (D-95). |

CMP-13 Connection banner and Low Signal chip
| Part or state | Specification |
| Offline banner | Full width, directly under the top bar of the chat list (AND-03, WEB-04) and of the chat (AND-05, WEB-06); height 40; fill color.info.bg; status.offline at icon.md and "Offline: messages send when connected" in body.sm color.info.text, padding 16. Shown while the device or browser reports no connection (D-12, LS-5 "No connection" row). |
| "waiting for Wi-Fi" banner | Android only (D-19). Same size and place as the offline banner, below it if both apply; fill color.warning.bg; status.waitingWifi and "<n> waiting for Wi-Fi" (singular "1 waiting for Wi-Fi") in body.sm color.warning.text. Shown on the chat list while n items are deferred under LS-5 (AND-03); in a chat it is shown when that chat has deferred items, counting only them. |
| Low Signal chip | Android only (D-19). In the chat list top bar, at the right: height 28, padding 4 by 10, radius.full, fill color.warning.bg, status.lowSignal at icon.sm and "Low Signal" in label.sm color.warning.text. Shown while the Automatic or Forced row of LS-5 applies (AND-03). A tap opens Settings at the Force Low Signal Mode switch. |

| Disabled | N/A: banners reflect state and are not controls. |
| Loading | N/A: the banners show state, not progress. |
| Error | N/A: the banners are not errors; failed items use CMP-08. |

CMP-14 Folder tabs
| Part or state | Specification |

Tabs: "All", then the user's folders in creation order (AND-03). Each tab: label.md, padding 16 horizontal, one line, maximum width 160 with ellipsis. After the last tab: an icon button with action.folder and label "Folders" that opens Folders (AND-13, WEB-13). The row is shown even when the user has no folders, with "All" and the button. |
| Default | Inactive tab: label color.text.secondary. Active tab: label color.text.primary and a 2 high color.brand.primary indicator under the label, radius 2 at the top; the indicator slides between tabs over motion.duration.base. |
| Pressed | Tab fill color.bg.pressed. |
| Focused | 2 px color.focus.ring outline around the tab; arrow keys move between tabs (role tablist and tab). |
| Disabled | N/A: tabs filter local data and work offline. |
| Loading | N/A: folders come from the local cache. |
| Error | N/A: a folder that no longer exists after a resync is removed and "All" becomes active. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-15 Bottom sheet
| Part or state | Specification |
| Layout | Slides up from the bottom over color.scrim; fill color.bg.elevated; elevation.3; top corners radius.xl; handle 32 by 4, radius.full, color.border.strong, 8 from the top; optional title in title.md and subtitle in body.sm color.text.secondary with padding 16; maximum height 90% of the screen, the content scrolls. Item: height 56, padding 16, icon.lg at the left in color.text.primary, label in body.lg; a danger item (Delete, Leave group, Sign out) uses color.danger for icon and label. Bottom padding includes the system navigation inset. |
| Used by | The New sheet ("New chat" with action.newChat and "New group" with action.newGroup, D-142), chat row actions (AND-03), Attach menu (AND-08), Mute options (1 hour, 8 hours, 1 week, Until I unmute; D-109), Add to folder (a checklist of folders), Theme (System, Light, Dark with a check on the current one), self-destruct timer (V2, AND-19), restrict options (V2, AND-20). |
| Enter and exit | Enter: slide up over motion.duration.slow with motion.easing.enter and fade the scrim in. Exit: motion.duration.base with motion.easing.exit. Dismiss by a tap on the scrim, the Android back button, Escape on web, or a downward drag of more than 30% of its height. |
| Default | As above. |
| Pressed | Item fill color.bg.pressed. |
| Focused | Web below 768 px: focus moves to the first item on open, Tab cycles inside the sheet, and focus returns to the control that opened it on close. Item focus: inset 2 px color.focus.ring. |
| Disabled | Item label and icon color.text.disabled, with the reason on a second line in body.sm color.text.secondary ("No connection"). |
| Loading | An item whose action runs (Leave group, Archive) shows a 20 spinner at its right and the sheet stays open until it finishes, then closes. |
| Error | The sheet closes and a toast shows the error text with Retry. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-16 Dialog
| Part or state | Specification |
| Confirm dialog | Centered, width the screen minus 48 and at most 400; fill color.bg.elevated; elevation.3; radius.xl; padding 24; title in title.md; body in body.md color.text.secondary, 8 below the title; buttons right-aligned 24 below the body, 8 apart (Cancel as Secondary, the action as Primary or Danger). |
| Form dialog (web) | From 768 px: width 520, maximum height 80% of the window, a header with the title and nav.close, scrolling content, and a footer with the buttons. Below 768 px the same content is a full-screen page with a top bar (CMP-21). Used for New chat, New group, Forward, Folders and Settings (11.3). |
| Confirm texts | "Delete for everyone?" with Cancel and Delete (AND-06); "Leave <group title>?" with "Ownership passes to another member." when the user is the owner (D-91), Cancel and Leave; "Delete folder <name>?" with "The chats stay in All." (D-94), Cancel and Delete; Sign out with unsent items: "Sign out?" and "<n> unsent messages will be lost" (D-109), Cancel and Sign out; Discard: "Discard this message?" with Cancel and Discard. |
| Enter and exit | Fade and scale from 0.96 to 1 over motion.duration.slow with motion.easing.enter; exit over motion.duration.base. |
| Default | As above. |
| Pressed | Buttons per CMP-01. |
| Focused | Focus moves into the dialog on open (the first field, or the Cancel button of a confirm dialog), Tab is trapped inside, Escape closes, and focus returns to the opener (aria-modal="true", role="dialog" or "alertdialog" for confirms). |
| Disabled | The confirming button is disabled until the form is valid. |
| Loading | The confirming button shows its loading state; Cancel and Escape stay available for a form dialog and are disabled for a destructive action in progress. |
| Error | Field errors per CMP-02; an action error shows above the buttons as a block with fill color.danger.bg, status.failed and the text in body.sm color.danger, and Retry when the action can be repeated. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-17 Toast
| Part or state | Specification |
| Layout | Bottom-center, 16 above the composer or the bottom inset, width fit-content between 240 and 480 (screen width minus 32 below 512); fill color.toast.bg; elevation.2; radius.md; padding 12 vertical and 16 horizontal; text body.md color.toast.text, up to 2 lines; an optional Text button in color.toast.action at the right ("Retry"). |
| Behavior | One toast at a time; a new toast replaces the current one. Stays motion.toast.duration (4000 ms, or 6000 ms with an action); pauses while hovered or focused on web. Enter: slide up 16 and fade over motion.duration.slow; exit: fade over motion.duration.base. Announced politely (role="status", accessibilityLiveRegion polite). |
| Strings | "Copied" (Copy text); "Forwarded" (D-133); "Folder created"; "Password changed"; error texts of the strings module with "Retry" (D-95); the refusal texts of CMP-11 and CMP-10. |
| Default | As above. |
| Pressed | Action button per CMP-01 Text on the toast fill. |
| Focused | The action button is reachable by Tab while the toast is shown; focus does not move to the toast by itself. |
| Disabled | N/A: a toast has no disabled state. |
| Loading | N/A: a toast reports a result, not progress. |
| Error | An error toast has the same look with the text of the error; color is not used to signal it, the text does. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-18 Skeleton
| Part or state | Specification |
| Rule | Shown only when a wait passes motion.delay.loading (300 ms, D-95) and nothing cached can be shown; announced as "Loading" once (accessibilityLabel on the container, aria-busy="true"). |
| Blocks | Rectangles and circles in color.skeleton.base, radius.sm (circles radius.full), with a highlight band in color.skeleton.highlight sweeping left to right every motion.duration.shimmer; static with reduced motion. |
| Chat list | 8 rows: 56 circle, a 40% wide by 14 block and a 70% wide by 12 block, matching CMP-09 spacing. |
| Chat | 6 bubbles alternating sides, widths 60%, 40%, 70%, 30%, 55%, 45% of the maximum bubble width, heights 36 or 56, radius.bubble. |
| Admin table | 10 rows of the table's column widths, block height 12. |
| Pressed, Focused, Disabled, Error | N/A: a skeleton is not interactive and is replaced by content or the error state. |
| Loading | The skeleton is itself the loading state. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-19 Empty state
| Part or state | Specification |
| Layout | Centered in the available area, maximum width 320, top padding space.9: a 96 circle in color.bg.surface with an icon.xl icon in color.text.secondary, the title in title.md color.text.primary 16 below it, the body in body.md color.text.secondary 8 below, and an optional Primary button 24 below. |
| Instances | Chat with no messages (F3): icon action.send, "No messages yet", "Send the first message." Archive (F9): action.archive, "No archived chats", "Chats you archive appear here." Folder with no chats (F9): action.folder, "No chats in this folder", button "Add chats". Forward or folder picker search with no match: action.search, "No chats match". New chat with no result: "No user with that username" (AND-10) as the result line, not a full empty state. Web right pane with no chat open: action.newChat, "Select a chat" (11.3), "Choose a chat or start a new one.", button "New chat" (D-142). Admin audit log: admin.audit, "No actions yet" (F12). Admin accounts filter with no match: admin.accounts, "No accounts match". |
| Note | The chat list is never empty, because Saved Messages is always listed (F2). |
| Default | As above. |
| Pressed, Focused, Disabled, Loading | The button per CMP-01; the empty state itself is not interactive. |
| Error | N/A: a failed load uses CMP-20, never an empty state. |
---TABLE END---

CMP-20 Error state
| Part or state | Specification |
| Layout | As CMP-19, with status.failed in color.danger in a 96 color.danger.bg circle, the title in title.md, the body in body.md color.text.secondary and a Primary button "Retry" when the action can be repeated (D-95). Used when a screen has nothing cached to show. |
| Load errors | Title "Couldn't load"; body: the error text from the strings module, for example "Check your connection and try again." (network), "Too many requests. Try again in <n> seconds." (DG429, D-77), "You don't have access to this chat." (RLS denial, the chat is then removed from the list after resync, D-91). |
| Permission denied (OS) | Shown in a dialog (CMP-16) when an Android permission is denied, so the action does not start (F5): camera "DoloGram needs camera access to take photos and videos.", microphone "DoloGram needs microphone access to record voice notes.", media "DoloGram needs access to your photos and videos to attach them.", location (V2) "DoloGram needs your location to share it." Buttons: Cancel and "Open settings". On web the browser's own prompt is used and a denial shows the same text in a toast. |
| Default | As above. |
| Pressed, Focused, Disabled | The Retry button per CMP-01. |
| Loading | Retry shows its loading state; when the load succeeds the content replaces the error state. |
| Error | The error state is itself the error presentation. |
---TABLE END---

12.5 Screen Templates and Layouts
Templates name the components of 12.4 from top to bottom. A screen ID refers to Section 11; the template applies to both clients unless it says otherwise. Every template has the loading, empty, error and offline states of 3.5 and 11.6 through CMP-18, CMP-19, CMP-20 and CMP-13.

Ask the owner" in body.sm color.text.secondary centered (D-47). No sign-up or phone option (D-04). | Loading: the button. Offline: Sign in disabled with "No connection" under it. |

12.6 Navigation Maps
The chat list is home on both user clients. Navigation follows Instagram (D-141, D-142): on Android and on the web below 768 px a bottom tab bar (CMP-32) with Chats, New and Profile on the root screens; on the web from 768 px a navigation rail (CMP-33) with the same items. New opens New chat or New group; Profile opens Settings. The admin web uses the left sidebar of 11.4.
Android app (expo-router tabs and stack)
| Screen | Route file in apps/mobile/app | Reached from | Leaves to | Presentation |
| AND-01 Sign in | sign-in.tsx | First launch, Sign out | AND-02 or AND-03 (replace) | Stack root while signed out |
| AND-02 Change password | change-password.tsx | AND-01 when forced; AND-14 | AND-03 (replace) or back | Push; no back while forced |
| AND-03 Chat list | (main)/(tabs)/index.tsx | Sign-in, the Chats tab, every back | AND-04, AND-05, AND-13, the New sheet, AND-14 | Tab root (Chats) while signed in |
| New sheet | Not a route (sheet) | The New tab | AND-10, AND-11 | Bottom sheet |
| AND-04 Archive | (main)/archive.tsx | AND-03 "Archived (n)" row | AND-05 | Push |
| AND-05 Chat | chat/[chatId]/index.tsx | AND-03, AND-04, AND-10, AND-11 | AND-06, AND-07, AND-08, AND-09, AND-12 | Push |
| AND-06 Message actions | Not a route (sheet) | Long press on a bubble | AND-07 or back to AND-05 | Bottom sheet |
| AND-07 Forward picker | forward.tsx | AND-06 Forward | Back to AND-05 after forwarding | Push (modal presentation) |
| AND-08 Attach menu | Not a route (sheet) | AND-05 Attach | System pickers, back to AND-05 | Bottom sheet |
| AND-09 Media viewer | chat/[chatId]/media/[mediaId].tsx | A tap on a tile | Back | Push, fade, full screen |
| AND-10 New chat | new-chat.tsx | The New sheet | AND-05 (replace) | Push |
| AND-11 New group | new-group.tsx | The New sheet | AND-05 (replace) | Push |
| AND-12 Chat info | chat/[chatId]/info.tsx | AND-05 header | Back; AND-03 after Leave | Push |
| AND-13 Folders | folders.tsx | AND-14, folder tabs button | Back | Push |
| AND-14 Settings | (main)/(tabs)/settings.tsx | The Profile tab, Low Signal chip | AND-02, AND-13, AND-01 after Sign out | Tab root (Profile) |
| AND-15 to AND-24 (V2, V3) | Sheets inside AND-05, or settings/<name>.tsx and call/[chatId].tsx | As 11.2 | As 11.2 | As 11.2; the incoming call is a full-screen modal |
---TABLE END---

The Android back button closes the top sheet, overlay or dialog first, then pops the stack; on AND-02 while forced it does nothing, on AND-14 it returns to the Chats tab, and on AND-03 it leaves the app (11.2).
Screen push uses the native stack slide over motion.duration.slow; with reduced motion the screen appears without a slide.
Assumption: expo-router, with its Tabs layout and a custom tab bar component, is included with the Expo SDK pinned at M0-08 and works in the development build; checked at M0-08; fallback @react-navigation/bottom-tabs and @react-navigation/native-stack (both MIT) with the same screen names and the same map (D-142).
User web app (Next.js App Router)
| Screen | Route in apps/web/src/app | Below 768 px | From 768 px |
| WEB-01 Sign in | (auth)/sign-in/page.tsx | Full page | Full page, centered column |
| WEB-02 Change password | (auth)/change-password/page.tsx | Full page | Full page |
| WEB-03, WEB-04 Main layout and chat list | (main)/layout.tsx and (main)/page.tsx | The chat list fills the screen, tab bar (CMP-32) below | Rail (CMP-33), chat list pane, "Select a chat" right |
| Folder filter | ?folder=<folder_id> on the chat list routes | Tabs above the list | Tabs above the list pane |
| WEB-05 Archive | (main)/archive/page.tsx | Full screen list with back | Archive replaces the list in the left pane, with back |
| WEB-06 Chat | (main)/chat/[chatId]/page.tsx | The chat fills the screen with no tab bar, back to / | Rail, list, chat |
| WEB-12 Chat info | ?info=1 on the chat route | Full screen with back | Right pane: replaces the chat from 768 to 1279 px, third column from 1280 px |
| WEB-07 Message actions | No route | Message focus overlay (CMP-34) | Hover row, palette popover and menu (CMP-23) |
| WEB-08, WEB-11, WEB-13, WEB-14 Forward, New chat, New group, Folders, Settings | No route (dialog state) | Full-screen dialog | Form dialog 520 wide |
| WEB-10 Media viewer | No route (overlay state) | Full-screen overlay | Full-screen overlay |
---TABLE END---

The browser back button closes an open dialog or overlay first (each pushes a history entry with the same URL and listens for popstate), then goes to the previous route.
Signed-out visits to (main) routes redirect to /sign-in; a session with must_change_password redirects every route to /change-password (D-78).
Keyboard: Escape closes the top layer; Tab follows the visual order: rail, top bar, banners, folder tabs, list, then the chat (top bar, messages, composer); below 768 px the tab bar comes last (D-95).
Admin web (Next.js App Router, apps/admin)
| Screen | Route in apps/admin/src/app | Reached from | Leaves to |
| ADM-01 Unlock | unlock/page.tsx | Any route without a valid session cookie; Lock now | ADM-02 |
| ADM-02 Overview | page.tsx (/) | Unlock, sidebar | ADM-06 from a maintenance row, ADM-08 from the settings summary |
| ADM-03 Accounts | accounts/page.tsx | Sidebar | ADM-04, ADM-05 |
| ADM-04 Create account | accounts/new/page.tsx and accounts/import/page.tsx | ADM-03 Create account, Import CSV | ADM-03 |
| ADM-05 Account detail | accounts/[userId]/page.tsx | An ADM-03 row | ADM-03 |
| ADM-06 Maintenance | maintenance/page.tsx | Sidebar, ADM-02 | None |
| ADM-07 Audit log | audit/page.tsx | Sidebar | None |
| ADM-08 Settings check | settings-check/page.tsx | Sidebar, ADM-02 | None |
---TABLE END---

Admin shell: a 240 wide left sidebar (fill color.bg.surface) with "DoloGram Admin" in title.md at the top and the items Overview, Accounts, Maintenance, Audit log and Settings check (40 high, icon.md and body.md, the current one with fill color.bg.selected and color.brand.primary text, aria-current="page"); a 56 high header with the project name in title.md and the Secondary button "Lock now" (admin.lock) at the right (11.4); the content area with padding 32 and a maximum width of 1200.
12.7 Responsive Rules
| Client and width | Layout | Source |
| Android phones, any width | One column; portrait only (orientation "portrait" in app.json); side padding 16; bubbles at most 78% of the list width; no tablet layout (D-35). | D-35, D-135 |
| User web below 768 px | One column: the chat list with the bottom tab bar (CMP-32), then the chat with a back button and no tab bar (D-35); side padding 16; dialogs and sheets are full screen; message actions use the message focus overlay (CMP-34); touch targets 44. | D-35, 11.3, D-142 |
| User web 768 to 1279 px | Navigation rail 72 wide (CMP-33), then two columns: list pane fixed at 360 with a 1 px color.border.subtle right border, chat pane fills the rest; chat info replaces the chat in the right pane with a back button; dialogs centered (520 wide for forms, 400 for confirms). | D-35, D-145 |
| User web from 1280 px | Rail 72, list pane 400; chat pane fills the rest; chat info opens as a third column of 360 at the right with its own 1 px left border; the message column is centered inside the chat pane with a maximum width of 840. | D-145 |
| Admin web | Desktop browser on the owner's computer; designed for widths from 1024 px; below 1024 the sidebar collapses to a 64 wide icon rail with tooltips showing the item names; tables scroll horizontally inside their card. | D-102, D-135 |
---TABLE END---

Breakpoint test: at 767 px the user web is one column and at 768 px two (T-40).
The breakpoint is a CSS media query (min-width: 768px) and (min-width: 1280px) in the layout module; no JavaScript width check decides the layout, so the first paint is correct.
Safe areas: Android uses react-native-safe-area-context insets for the status and navigation bars; web uses env(safe-area-inset-bottom) under the composer on phones.
Text never truncates inside message bubbles; names and previews truncate with an ellipsis on one line.
12.8 Accessibility Defaults
Accessibility uses platform defaults (D-54), made checkable by D-95. This section fixes the values those checks use.
| Topic | Rule | Check |
| Labels | Every interactive element has an accessibility label (accessibilityLabel on Android, visible text or aria-label on web). Icon-only buttons and the text button "Send" use the label written next to them in 12.3 or 12.4 ("Chats", "New", "Profile and settings", "Camera", "Attach", "React", "More actions", "Record voice note", "Cancel reply", "Remove <file name>", "Jump to newest message"). Bubbles, rows and chips use the label patterns of CMP-07, CMP-09 and CMP-12. | eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y (web); render test listing pressables without accessibilityLabel (Android) (D-95) |
| Roles and states | Buttons role button, switches role switch with checked, tabs role tab with selected, dialogs role dialog or alertdialog, menus role menu, banners role status, the tab bar role tablist, the rail a nav landmark. Disabled controls expose disabled; loading buttons expose busy. | Same checks |
| Contrast | Text at least 4.5:1 and non-text UI parts at least 3:1 in both themes; the pairs of 12.2 are the checked set; color is never the only signal (statuses have icons and words, usage bars have percentages, pills have text). | Contrast test of 12.10 (D-95) |
| Focus order | Web: Tab follows the visual order (12.6); dialogs and sheets trap focus and return it to the opener; the focus ring of 12.4 is always visible on keyboard focus; a "Skip to messages" link is the first focusable element of the chat route and appears on focus. Android: TalkBack order follows the layout order; sheets move accessibility focus to their title. | T-40 manual Tab pass |
| Touch targets | Android: at least 48 by 48 dp for every touch target, using hitSlop where the visual is smaller (icon buttons, the composer tile remove button, reaction chips at 28 high get vertical hitSlop of 10). User web: at least 44 by 44 px. Admin web: at least 32 by 32 px, above the 24 px minimum of WCAG 2.2 (D-136). | Render test measures pressable bounds (Android); review at M4-03 |
| Text size | System font scale on Android and browser zoom to 200% on web keep every function usable; caps of 12.2 apply only to bars. | Manual pass at M4-03 |
| Motion | Reduced motion rules of 12.2. | Manual pass at M4-03 |
| Announcements | New incoming messages in the open chat are announced politely ("<sender>: <text>") while the screen reader is on; a status change of an own message to failed is announced ("Message failed"). | Manual pass at M4-03 |
---TABLE END---

12.9 Admin Web Components
The admin web uses the tokens and the shared components of 12.4 in the admin sizes (CMP-01 36 high, CMP-02 36 high, targets 32) and adds these components for the screens of 6.6 and 11.4. It follows the system theme (D-109) and never shows message content (D-103).

Body rows 48 high, body.md color.text.primary, 1 px color.border.subtle between rows, padding 12 horizontal. 12.10 Implementation Mapping
One token set and one component specification serve three apps. Tokens are defined once in TypeScript in packages/shared; Android reads them through a React context, the web apps through CSS custom properties generated from the same file. Component names are identical on every client, so a defect report naming CMP-07 MessageBubble points to one file per app (D-138).
Naming
| Thing | Convention | Example |
| Token | Dot name in this section; the TypeScript path is the same; the CSS custom property is --dg- plus the dot name with dots replaced by hyphens. | color.bg.app -> tokens.color.bg.app -> --dg-color-bg-app |
| Theme values | Color tokens have a light and a dark value: tokens.color is { light, dark }; other tokens have one value. | tokens.color.light.bg.app = "#FFFFFF" |
| Component | PascalCase name from the table below, one component per file, the same props on every client where the behavior is the same. | MessageBubble |
| Android component file | apps/mobile/src/components/<Name>.tsx with styles from makeStyles(theme) in the same file. | apps/mobile/src/components/MessageBubble.tsx |
| Web component file | apps/web/src/components/<Name>/<Name>.tsx and <Name>.module.css (CSS Modules, built into Next.js); class names camelCase. | apps/web/src/components/MessageBubble/MessageBubble.module.css |
| Admin component file | apps/admin/src/components/<Name>/<Name>.tsx and <Name>.module.css. | apps/admin/src/components/DataTable/DataTable.tsx |
| Icon | Semantic name from 12.3 passed to the Icon component; never a Lucide import in a screen. | <Icon name="status.queued" size="xs" /> |
| Strings | Keys in the strings module grouped by screen, camelCase. | strings.chat.emptyTitle = "No messages yet" |
---TABLE END---

Tokens
| Token group | Source (packages/shared) | React Native (Expo) | Next.js (apps/web, apps/admin) |
| Color | src/theme/tokens.ts: color.light and color.dark | apps/mobile/src/theme/ThemeProvider.tsx picks light or dark from the Theme setting (AsyncStorage key dg.theme, D-83) and useColorScheme(); useTheme() returns the active values | src/theme/tokens.css generated by npm run tokens: :root holds light values, [data-theme="dark"] dark values; apps/web sets data-theme on <html> from localStorage dg.theme in an inline script before first paint, and System follows @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark); apps/admin uses the media query only (D-109) |
| Typography | src/theme/tokens.ts: type.<token> { family, weight, size, lineHeight, letterSpacing } | Fonts loaded with expo-font and @expo-google-fonts/inter and @expo-google-fonts/jetbrains-mono; fontFamily names Inter_400Regular, Inter_500Medium, Inter_600SemiBold, Inter_700Bold, JetBrainsMono_400Regular, one per weight | next/font/google Inter and JetBrains_Mono with display swap and the latin subset, self-hosted at build time, exposed as --dg-font-sans and --dg-font-mono; sizes in rem |
| Spacing, radius | src/theme/tokens.ts: space, radius | Numbers in StyleSheet values | --dg-space-*, --dg-radius-* in px |
| Elevation | src/theme/tokens.ts: elevation.n { android, webLight, webDark } | elevation and shadowColor #000000 | --dg-elevation-n, with the dark value under [data-theme="dark"] |
| Motion | src/theme/tokens.ts: motion | Animated API with useNativeDriver true; AccessibilityInfo.isReduceMotionEnabled() switches durations | CSS transition values from --dg-motion-*; @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) overrides |
| Z-index | src/theme/tokens.ts: z | zIndex for siblings; Modal or a root portal for z.sheet and above | --dg-z-* |
| Icons | src/theme/icons.ts: semantic name to Lucide name | apps/mobile/src/components/Icon.tsx with lucide-react-native and react-native-svg | Icon.tsx in each web app with lucide-react |
| Emoji palette | src/config (M2-01): reactionPalette and extraReactionPalette | ReactionPalette reads it | ReactionPalette reads it |
| Strings | src/strings (D-35, D-95) | Imported | Imported by apps/web; apps/admin has src/strings.ts of its own (11.6) |
---TABLE END---

Components
| ID | Component name | React Native (Expo) primitive | Next.js primitive | Admin |
| CMP-01 | Button, IconButton | Pressable with accessibilityRole button | <button type="button"> | Yes |
| CMP-02 | TextField | TextInput | <input> or <textarea> with <label> | Yes |
| CMP-03 | SearchField | TextInput with accessibilityRole search | <input type="search"> | Yes |

| CMP-06 | Badge | View and Text | <span> | No |

| CMP-08 | MessageStatus | Icon with accessibilityLabel | Icon with visually hidden text | No |

| CMP-17 | Toast, ToastProvider | Root-level View in a portal | Root-level <div role="status"> | Yes |

| CMP-22 | DateSeparator, UnreadDivider, JumpToBottom | View, Pressable | <div role="separator">, <button> | No |
| CMP-23 | Menu | N/A: Android uses CMP-15 | Popover <div role="menu"> positioned in a portal | Yes |
| CMP-24 to CMP-31 | DataTable, FilterBar, AdminForm, ConfirmDialog, AuditView, UsageBar and JobPanel, UnavailablePanel, StatusPill | N/A: admin only | N/A: admin only | apps/admin/src/components |
| CMP-32 | TabBar | Custom tabBar component of the expo-router Tabs layout, Pressable items with accessibilityRole tab | <nav> fixed to the bottom with <a> items and a <button> for New | No |
| CMP-33 | NavRail | N/A: web only | <nav aria-label="Main"> in (main)/layout.tsx | No |
| CMP-34 | MessageFocusOverlay | React Native Modal (transparent) with Animated scale and opacity | <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true"> in a portal | No |
---TABLE END---

Checks that keep the three apps identical
Contrast test: packages/shared/src/theme/contrast.test.ts (Vitest, D-100) computes every pair of 12.2 for both themes and fails below 4.5:1 for text and 3:1 for non-text (D-95, T-40).
Token sync test: a Vitest test regenerates tokens.css in memory and fails if the committed file differs, so web and Android cannot drift.
No color literals: an ESLint no-restricted-syntax rule rejects string literals matching #[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8} or rgb( and rgba( in apps/mobile, apps/web and apps/admin, and Stylelint color-no-hex rejects them in .module.css files; packages/shared/src/theme is the only exception (M4-02).
Accessibility: eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y on apps/web and apps/admin; the Android render test of D-95 lists pressables without accessibilityLabel.
Fonts and icons are bundled at build time; a network inspector on Android shows no font or icon request.
Assumption: @expo-google-fonts/inter and @expo-google-fonts/jetbrains-mono install on the Expo SDK pinned at M0-08; checked at M4-02; fallback the TTF files from the Inter and JetBrains Mono releases (both SIL Open Font License 1.1) placed in apps/mobile/assets/fonts and loaded with expo-font under the same family names.
Implementation: M4-02 builds the tokens, theme providers, icon map and checks; M4-03 builds the state components, the tab bar, rail and focus overlay, and applies 12.7 and 12.8; the screen tasks of 10.10 build the components of their screens; M4-08 builds the admin components of 12.9.
